MATH RUN · SOUND PROMPTS
========================

The easiest way: open the Studio, click "Sounds", pick a theme or pet and press Start.
The Studio copies each prompt and catches the file you download from the sound
generator (it watches your Downloads folder), then trims it, evens out the volume
and adds soft fades. No renaming, no dragging.

Generator tips:
 - Set the length yourself (not Auto, which tends to make 7-second clips): see the
   length per sound below. A long clip still works: the Studio keeps the best hit.
 - Most generators make several versions: listen and download only the nicest.
 - For the tap sound, download up to 3 versions: the game rotates between them.
 - Check the licence: the sounds must be allowed in a paid app.

What each sound is for:
 - Number key tap (generate at the shortest length (0.5 s)): every key press (a clip with several taps gives up to 3 variations)
 - Backspace (generate at the shortest length (0.5 s)): deleting a digit
 - Menu button (generate at the shortest length (0.5 s)): buttons outside the keypad
 - Correct answer (generate at 0.5–1 s): every right answer; the game plays it higher for each answer in a streak, so it must be ONE note
 - Wrong answer (generate at about 1 s): a wrong answer; must be kind, never a buzzer
 - New best level (generate at 1–1.5 s): passing your best level during a run
 - Fanfare (generate at 2–3 s): end of a run, unlocking a theme or pet
 - Coin (generate at the shortest length (0.5 s)): coins counting up on the results screen (plays many times in a row)
 - Gem (generate at 0.5–1 s): earning a gem
 - Pet voice (generate at 2–3 s): a pet talking; the Studio cuts the clip into separate syllables


################################################################
# 1. DEFAULT SOUNDS (used by Sunny and every theme without its own)
################################################################

DEFAULT SOUNDS
   Keys feel like: a soft, rounded wooden toy block · Instrument: a warm marimba

   Number key tap (generate at the shortest length (0.5 s)):
   One soft, short, muted tap on a soft, rounded wooden toy block, like pressing a well-made button. Gentle and satisfying, very short. Only one tap, then silence. Isolated sound that starts from silence, clean close recording, nothing else playing, no background noise, no clicks, no crackle, no static, no echo, soft and pleasant, never harsh or shrill.

   Backspace (generate at the shortest length (0.5 s)):
   One soft, slightly lower and duller tap on a soft, rounded wooden toy block, a gentle "tuk" for undoing something. Very short. Only one tap, then silence. Isolated sound that starts from silence, clean close recording, nothing else playing, no background noise, no clicks, no crackle, no static, no echo, soft and pleasant, never harsh or shrill.

   Menu button (generate at the shortest length (0.5 s)):
   One soft, round, friendly pop on a soft, rounded wooden toy block, like a light bubble or cork. Short. Only one pop, then silence. Isolated sound that starts from silence, clean close recording, nothing else playing, no background noise, no clicks, no crackle, no static, no echo, soft and pleasant, never harsh or shrill.

   Correct answer (generate at 0.5–1 s):
   One single warm, round note on a warm marimba, played softly (with a soft felt mallet if it is struck), a rounded attack and a short natural fade. Only one note, no melody, no chord. Isolated sound that starts from silence, clean close recording, nothing else playing, no background noise, no clicks, no crackle, no static, no echo, soft and pleasant, never harsh or shrill.

   Wrong answer (generate at about 1 s):
   A soft, kind "oops": two gentle low notes stepping down on a warm marimba, muted and short. Not a buzzer, not sad, not harsh. Isolated sound that starts from silence, clean close recording, nothing else playing, no background noise, no clicks, no crackle, no static, no echo, soft and pleasant, never harsh or shrill.

   New best level (generate at 1–1.5 s):
   A short, cheerful rising run of four quick notes on a warm marimba, ending on a bright note. About 1 second. Isolated sound that starts from silence, clean close recording, nothing else playing, no background noise, no clicks, no crackle, no static, no echo, soft and pleasant, never harsh or shrill.

   Fanfare (generate at 2–3 s):
   A short, happy celebration jingle on a warm marimba with a light sparkle at the end, about 2 seconds, ending cleanly. Cute, for a children's game, not loud. Isolated sound that starts from silence, clean close recording, nothing else playing, no background noise, no clicks, no crackle, no static, no echo, soft and pleasant, never harsh or shrill.

   Coin (generate at the shortest length (0.5 s)):
   One tiny, soft, high "ting" of a small coin, with a hint of a warm marimba. Very short and delicate. Only one ting, then silence. Isolated sound that starts from silence, clean close recording, nothing else playing, no background noise, no clicks, no crackle, no static, no echo, soft and pleasant, never harsh or shrill.

   Gem (generate at 0.5–1 s):
   A small magical sparkle: a soft shimmer with one bright bell-like ping on a warm marimba. About 0.7 seconds. Isolated sound that starts from silence, clean close recording, nothing else playing, no background noise, no clicks, no crackle, no static, no echo, soft and pleasant, never harsh or shrill.

   Pet voice (generate at 2–3 s):
   A cute cartoon baby creature voice (a cute little round creature) babbling happy gibberish: about 6 short, separate syllables with a clear little pause between each, like "ba... mi... doo... ki... pa... no". High, sweet and playful voice, friendly and cheerful. No real words, no music, no background noise, no echo.
